1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a metabolism algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 175. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 443/3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the metabolism decomposition. By minimality, the element a^175 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 443-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
